    (function() {\n
        'use strict';\n
\n
        // Prevent multiple initializations\n
        if (window.__ictserveThemeInit) return;\n
        window.__ictserveThemeInit = true;\n
\n
        const STORAGE_KEY = 'theme';\n
        const DEFAULT_THEME = 'light'; // Immutable default per v3.6.0\n
\n
        /**\n
         * Get theme from localStorage (simple string format)\n
         * @returns {string|null} Theme value or null\n
         */\n
        function getStoredTheme() {\n
            try {\n
                const value = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);\n
                return value === 'dark' ? 'dark' : (value === 'light' ? 'light' : null);\n
            } catch (e) {\n
                return null;\n
            }\n
        }\n
\n
        /**\n
         * Store theme in localStorage (simple string format)\n
         * @param {string} theme - Theme value ('light' or 'dark')\n
         */\n
        function setStoredTheme(theme) {\n
            const normalized = theme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light';\n
            try {\n
                localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, normalized);\n
            } catch (e) {\n
                // Ignore storage errors (private browsing, quota exceeded)\n
            }\n
        }\n
\n
        /**\n
         * Apply theme to document root\n
         * @param {string} theme - Theme value ('light' or 'dark')\n
         */\n
        function applyTheme(theme) {\n
            const normalized = theme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light';\n
            const root = document.documentElement;\n
\n
            if (normalized === 'dark') {\n
                root.classList.add('dark');\n
                root.setAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');\n
            } else {\n
                root.classList.remove('dark');\n
                root.setAttribute('data-theme', 'light');\n
            }\n
\n
            setStoredTheme(normalized);\n
        }\n
\n
        // Initialize theme: stored > default (light)\n
        // v3.6.0: No system preference detection\n
        const initialTheme = getStoredTheme() || DEFAULT_THEME;\n
        applyTheme(initialTheme);\n
\n
        // Expose global theme API for components\n
        window.ICTServeTheme = {\n
            get: function() {\n
                return getStoredTheme() || DEFAULT_THEME;\n
            },\n
            set: function(theme) {\n
                applyTheme(theme);\n
                // Dispatch event for reactive components\n
                window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('theme-changed', {\n
                    detail: {\n
                        theme: theme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light'\n
                    }\n
                }));\n
            },\n
            toggle: function() {\n
                const current = this.get();\n
                const next = current === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';\n
                this.set(next);\n
                return next;\n
            }\n
        };\n
    })();\n

                localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, normalized);\n
            } catch (e) {\n
                // Ignore storage errors (private browsing, quota exceeded)\n
            }\n
        }\n
\n
        /**\n
         * Apply theme to document root\n
         * @param {string} theme - Theme value ('light' or 'dark')\n
         */\n
        function applyTheme(theme) {\n
            const normalized = theme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light';\n
            const root = document.documentElement;\n
\n
            if (normalized === 'dark') {\n
                root.classList.add('dark');\n
                root.setAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');\n
            } else {\n
                root.classList.remove('dark');\n
                root.setAttribute('data-theme', 'light');\n
            }\n
\n
            setStoredTheme(normalized);\n
        }\n
\n
        // Initialize theme: stored > default (light)\n
        // v3.6.0: No system preference detection\n
        const initialTheme = getStoredTheme() || DEFAULT_THEME;\n
        applyTheme(initialTheme);\n
\n
        // Expose global theme API for components\n
        window.ICTServeTheme = {\n
            get: function() {\n
                return getStoredTheme() || DEFAULT_THEME;\n
            },\n
            set: function(theme) {\n
                applyTheme(theme);\n
                // Dispatch event for reactive components\n
                window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('theme-changed', {\n
                    detail: {\n
                        theme: theme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light'\n
                    }\n
                }));\n
            },\n
            toggle: function() {\n
                const current = this.get();\n
                const next = current === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';\n
                this.set(next);\n
                return next;\n
            }\n
        };\n
    })();\n

            } catch (e) {\n
                // Ignore storage errors (private browsing, quota exceeded)\n
            }\n
        }\n
\n
        /**\n
         * Apply theme to document root\n
         * @param {string} theme - Theme value ('light' or 'dark')\n
         */\n
        function applyTheme(theme) {\n
            const normalized = theme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light';\n
            const root = document.documentElement;\n
\n
            if (normalized === 'dark') {\n
                root.classList.add('dark');\n
                root.setAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');\n
            } else {\n
                root.classList.remove('dark');\n
                root.setAttribute('data-theme', 'light');\n
            }\n
\n
            setStoredTheme(normalized);\n
        }\n
\n
        // Initialize theme: stored > default (light)\n
        // v3.6.0: No system preference detection\n
        const initialTheme = getStoredTheme() || DEFAULT_THEME;\n
        applyTheme(initialTheme);\n
\n
        // Expose global theme API for components\n
        window.ICTServeTheme = {\n
            get: function() {\n
                return getStoredTheme() || DEFAULT_THEME;\n
            },\n
            set: function(theme) {\n
                applyTheme(theme);\n
                // Dispatch event for reactive components\n
                window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('theme-changed', {\n
                    detail: {\n
                        theme: theme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light'\n
                    }\n
                }));\n
            },\n
            toggle: function() {\n
                const current = this.get();\n
                const next = current === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';\n
                this.set(next);\n
                return next;\n
            }\n
        };\n
    })();\n

                // Ignore storage errors (private browsing, quota exceeded)\n
            }\n
        }\n
\n
        /**\n
         * Apply theme to document root\n
         * @param {string} theme - Theme value ('light' or 'dark')\n
         */\n
        function applyTheme(theme) {\n
            const normalized = theme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light';\n
            const root = document.documentElement;\n
\n
            if (normalized === 'dark') {\n
                root.classList.add('dark');\n
                root.setAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');\n
            } else {\n
                root.classList.remove('dark');\n
                root.setAttribute('data-theme', 'light');\n
            }\n
\n
            setStoredTheme(normalized);\n
        }\n
\n
        // Initialize theme: stored > default (light)\n
        // v3.6.0: No system preference detection\n
        const initialTheme = getStoredTheme() || DEFAULT_THEME;\n
        applyTheme(initialTheme);\n
\n
        // Expose global theme API for components\n
        window.ICTServeTheme = {\n
            get: function() {\n
                return getStoredTheme() || DEFAULT_THEME;\n
            },\n
            set: function(theme) {\n
                applyTheme(theme);\n
                // Dispatch event for reactive components\n
                window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('theme-changed', {\n
                    detail: {\n
                        theme: theme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light'\n
                    }\n
                }));\n
            },\n
            toggle: function() {\n
                const current = this.get();\n
                const next = current === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';\n
                this.set(next);\n
                return next;\n
            }\n
        };\n
    })();\n

         * @param {string} theme - Theme value ('light' or 'dark')\n
         */\n
        function applyTheme(theme) {\n
            const normalized = theme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light';\n
            const root = document.documentElement;\n
\n
            if (normalized === 'dark') {\n
                root.classList.add('dark');\n
                root.setAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');\n
            } else {\n
                root.classList.remove('dark');\n
                root.setAttribute('data-theme', 'light');\n
            }\n
\n
            setStoredTheme(normalized);\n
        }\n
\n
        // Initialize theme: stored > default (light)\n
        // v3.6.0: No system preference detection\n
        const initialTheme = getStoredTheme() || DEFAULT_THEME;\n
        applyTheme(initialTheme);\n
\n
        // Expose global theme API for components\n
        window.ICTServeTheme = {\n
            get: function() {\n
                return getStoredTheme() || DEFAULT_THEME;\n
            },\n
            set: function(theme) {\n
                applyTheme(theme);\n
                // Dispatch event for reactive components\n
                window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('theme-changed', {\n
                    detail: {\n
                        theme: theme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light'\n
                    }\n
                }));\n
            },\n
            toggle: function() {\n
                const current = this.get();\n
                const next = current === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';\n
                this.set(next);\n
                return next;\n
            }\n
        };\n
    })();\n

                const next = current === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';\n
                this.set(next);\n
                return next;\n

        };\n

        /**\n
         * Get theme from localStorage (simple string format)\n
         * @returns {string|null} Theme value or null\n
         */\n
        function getStoredTheme() {\n
            try {\n
                const value = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);\n
                return value === 'dark' ? 'dark' : (value === 'light' ? 'light' : null);\n
            } catch (e) {\n
                return null;\n
            }\n
        }\n

        /**\n
         * Store theme in localStorage (simple string format)\n
         * @param {string} theme - Theme value ('light' or 'dark')\n

        function setStoredTheme(theme) {\n
            const normalized = theme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light';\n
            try {\n

            } catch (e) {\n
                // Ignore storage errors (private browsing, quota exceeded)\n
            }\n

\n
        /**\n
         * Get theme from localStorage (simple string format)\n
         * @returns {string|null} Theme value or null\n
         */\n
        function getStoredTheme() {\n
            try {\n
                const value = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);\n
                return value === 'dark' ? 'dark' : (value === 'light' ? 'light' : null);\n
            } catch (e) {\n
                return null;\n
            }\n

            const normalized = theme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light';\n

            } catch (e) {\n
                // Ignore storage errors (private browsing, quota exceeded)\n

        'use strict';\n

        // Prevent multiple initializations\n
        if (window.__ictserveThemeInit) return;\n
        window.__ictserveThemeInit = true;\n
\n
        const STORAGE_KEY = 'theme';\n
        const DEFAULT_THEME = 'light'; // Immutable default per v3.6.0\n
\n
        /**\n
         * Get theme from localStorage (simple string format)\n
         * @returns {string|null} Theme value or null\n
         */\n
        function getStoredTheme() {\n
            try {\n
                const value = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);\n
                return value === 'dark' ? 'dark' : (value === 'light' ? 'light' : null);\n
            } catch (e) {\n
                return null;\n
            }\n
        }\n
\n
        /**\n
         * Store theme in localStorage (simple string format)\n
         * @param {string} theme - Theme value ('light' or 'dark')\n
         */\n
        function setStoredTheme(theme) {\n
            const normalized = theme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light';\n
            try {\n
                localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, normalized);\n
            } catch (e) {\n
                // Ignore storage errors (private browsing, quota exceeded)\n
            }\n
        }\n
\n
        /**\n
         * Apply theme to document root\n
         * @param {string} theme - Theme value ('light' or 'dark')\n
         */\n
        function applyTheme(theme) {\n
            const normalized = theme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light';\n
            const root = document.documentElement;\n
\n
            if (normalized === 'dark') {\n
                root.classList.add('dark');\n
                root.setAttribute('data-theme', 'dark');\n
            } else {\n
                root.classList.remove('dark');\n
                root.setAttribute('data-theme', 'light');\n
            }\n
\n
            setStoredTheme(normalized);\n
        }\n
\n
        // Initialize theme: stored > default (light)\n
        // v3.6.0: No system preference detection\n
        const initialTheme = getStoredTheme() || DEFAULT_THEME;\n
        applyTheme(initialTheme);\n
\n
        // Expose global theme API for components\n
        window.ICTServeTheme = {\n
            get: function() {\n
                return getStoredTheme() || DEFAULT_THEME;\n

            set: function(theme) {\n
                applyTheme(theme);\n
                // Dispatch event for reactive components\n
                window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('theme-changed', {\n
